# SDD ledger — plan: docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-18-research-v2.md

Ruling BEFORE production verification/readiness: use fixed serial fixture profiles through the existing process supervisor, separately from the seven native implementation profiles. Require exact current source/test/lock identity, supervised argv/environment/closure and full fixed-file JUnit coverage with no skips/failures; supplied JUnit arithmetic alone grants no authority. Readiness must first reopen the actual native registry, then these final-source proofs and the fresh whole-branch review, binding the committed scientific execution snapshot. No permissive readiness stub or Boolean may satisfy it. Cost if wrong: freezing incomplete/changed code or confusing software fixtures with predictive/native qualification. All code and tests still precede final-source actual qualification/HPO; no method, support, analysis or resource deadline changes.

CLI integration found two preproduction defects, not scientific outcomes. The confirmation worker overwrote its argparse namespace with a dictionary; two inert metadata-programme RED cases reproduced AttributeError after successful return, and renaming the local variable passed both. Separately, the shared publication guard accepted only fresh JSON files while programme/observer/timing callers pass directories and the report passes Markdown; real qualified positive paths remain unrun. Ruling: explicitly type private publication outputs as JSON, Markdown or directory, retaining fresh/no-overwrite by default and allowing existing directories only for the existing identity-checked resume paths. All native/source/input guards remain before publication. Cost if wrong: unusable positive execution or unsafe overwrite; focused typed-output and protected-root rejection tests precede the change, and affected actual-negative/owned fixtures must pass. No data/model/support/analysis change.

Ruling BEFORE production CLI wiring: add a versioned practical-command control schema with a fixed whitelist of existing public operations. Control files contain metadata/references only; source binding, exact function arguments, output preservation and native gates remain enforced. `plan` describes work without granting execution; `run`/`freeze` call actual public implementations, and primary/secondary execution uses the existing owned cold observer with live resource admission, never a caller-supplied callback or arbitrary import. Output paths stay outside executable/data/proof inputs. Cost if wrong: an apparently convenient command could bypass qualification or lose paid outer work, so integration fixtures must exercise actual negative native gates and real immutable metadata outputs. No algorithm, support, model, deadline, analysis or protected-data change is admitted; final readiness/review and actual final-source qualification remain required.
